- **trading-ledger**: A trading journal that records the decision, not just the trade — the user reports a trade in plain language (e.g. "bought 500 NVDA at 135", "closed my TSLA position"), you parse it and write it to their Notion database, capturing the entry thesis, the plan, and the emotion; closing a trade updates the matching open row; review compares thesis vs outcome vs execution.
